  186:   assignment_id: string;
  187:   submission_type: SubmissionType;
  188:   body: string;
  189:   submitted_by: string;
  190: }): Promise<AssignmentSubmission> {
  191:   const db = getDb();
  192:   const existing = await db.assignment_submissions.where('assignment_id').equals(data.assignment_id).toArray();
  193:   const maxNo = existing.reduce((max, s) => Math.max(max, s.submission_no), 0);
  194:   const ts = now();
  195:   const sub: AssignmentSubmission = {
  196:     id: uuid(),
  197:     assignment_id: data.assignment_id,
  198:     submission_type: data.submission_type,
  199:     submission_no: maxNo + 1,
  200:     body: data.body,
  201:     submission_status: 'submitted',
  202:     submitted_by: data.submitted_by,
  203:     submitted_at: ts,
  204:     reviewed_by_user_id: null,
  205:     reviewed_at: null,
  206:     review_comment: null,
  207:   };
  208:   await db.assignment_submissions.put(sub);
  209:   return sub;
  210: }

   11: } from '../domain/taskNaviTypes';

   13:   COMPLETE_STATUSES, COMPLETE_BY_PURPOSE, progressLabel, ATTACHMENT_TYPE_LABELS,

   17: export function isEligibleActive(a: TaskAssignment): boolean {
   18:   return a.assignment_status === 'active' && a.eligibility_status === 'eligible';
   19: }
   20: 
   21: export function computeBadge(task: Task, assignments: TaskAssignment[], requirements: AssignmentDocumentRequirement[]): DisplayBadge | null {
   22:   if (task.status !== 'open') return null;
   23:   const eligible = assignments.filter(isEligibleActive);
   24: 
   25:   // 1. 期限超過
   26:   if (task.due_at && new Date(task.due_at) < new Date()) return 'overdue';
   27: 
   28:   // 2. 差戻し対応中
   29:   if (task.purpose === '作業依頼' && eligible.some((a) => a.progress_status === 'returned')) return 'returned';
   30: 
   31:   // 3. 受領待ち
   32:   if (task.purpose === '作業依頼' && eligible.some((a) => a.progress_status === 'reported')) return 'accept_pending';
   33: 
   34:   // 4. 確認待ち (requirement_status=required exists)
   35:   if (requirements.some((r) => r.requirement_status === 'required')) return 'confirm_pending';
   36: 
   37:   // 5. 回答待ち
   38:   if (task.purpose === '回答・申込受付' && eligible.some((a) => a.progress_status === 'pending')) return 'answer_pending';
   39: 
   40:   // 6. 対応中
   41:   return 'in_progress';
   42: }
   43: 
   44: export function computeBoardColumn(task: Task, badge: DisplayBadge | null): BoardColumn {
   45:   if (task.status === 'draft') return 'draft';
   46:   if (task.status === 'closed' || task.status === 'archived') return 'closed';
   47:   if (badge === 'confirm_pending' || badge === 'answer_pending') return 'confirm_answer';
   48:   if (badge === 'accept_pending' || badge === 'returned') return 'accept_return';
   49:   return 'in_progress';
   50: }
   51: 
   52: export function computeProgressPercent(assignments: TaskAssignment[]): number {
   53:   const eligible = assignments.filter(isEligibleActive);
   54:   if (eligible.length === 0) return 0;
   55:   const completed = eligible.filter((a) => COMPLETE_STATUSES.includes(a.progress_status)).length;
   56:   return Math.round((completed / eligible.length) * 100);
   57: }

   69: export async function loadAllTasksForUser(currentUserId: string): Promise<TaskView[]> {
   70:   const [allTasks, users, projects, myAssignments] = await Promise.all([
   71:     getAllTasks(), getUsers(), getProjects(), getAllAssignmentsByUser(currentUserId),
   72:   ]);
   73:   const userMap = new Map(users.map((u) => [u.id, u]));
   74:   const projectMap = new Map(projects.map((p) => [p.id, p]));
   75:   const myTaskIds = new Set(myAssignments.map((a) => a.task_id));
   76:   const relatedTasks = allTasks.filter((t) => {
   77:     if (t.created_by === currentUserId) return true;
   78:     if (t.successor_user_id === currentUserId) return true;
   79:     if (!myTaskIds.has(t.id)) return false;
   80:     const taskAssignments = myAssignments.filter((a) => a.task_id === t.id);
   81:     return taskAssignments.some(isEligibleActive);
   82:   });
   83:   const taskIds = relatedTasks.map((t) => t.id);
   84:   const allAssignments = await getAllAssignments(taskIds);
   85:   return relatedTasks
   86:     .filter((t) => t.status !== 'archived')
   87:     .map((task) => {
   88:       const assignments = allAssignments.filter((a) => a.task_id === task.id);
   89:       return buildTaskView(task, assignments, userMap, projectMap, currentUserId);
   90:     });
   91: }

  371: // === Document access & confirmation ===
  372: export async function accessAttachment(taskId: string, assignmentId: string, attachmentId: string, method: 'view' | 'download', userId: string): Promise<void> {
  373:   const task = await getTask(taskId);
  374:   if (!task) return;
  375:   const req = await getRequirement(assignmentId, attachmentId);
  376:   if (!req || req.requirement_status !== 'required') return;
  377:   const att = await getAttachment(attachmentId);
  378:   if (!att) return;
  379:   const ts = new Date().toISOString();
  380:   await updateRequirement(req.id, {
  381:     accessed_revision_no: att.current_revision_no,
  382:     accessed_at: ts,
  383:     access_method: method,
  384:   });
  385:   await addActivityEvent({
  386:     task_id: taskId, project_id: task.project_id,
  387:     event_type: method === 'view' ? 'attachment_viewed' : 'attachment_downloaded',
  388:     label: `資料「${att.name}」を${method === 'view' ? '閲覧' : 'ダウンロード'}しました`,
  389:     user_id: userId,
  390:   });

  492: export async function exemptAssignee(taskId: string, assignmentId: string, reason: string, userId: string): Promise<void> {
  493:   const task = await getTask(taskId);
  494:   if (!task) return;

  496:   const reqs = await getRequirementsByAssignment(assignmentId);

  499:       await updateRequirement(r.id, { requirement_status: 'not_required' });
  500:     }
  501:   }

  503:     task_id: taskId, project_id: task.project_id,
  504:     event_type: 'assignment_exempted', label: '対象者を免除しました',
  505:     user_id: userId, reason,
  506:   });
  507:   await checkAndAutoClose(taskId);

  174: export async function getAllSubmissionsByTask(taskId: string): Promise<AssignmentSubmission[]> {
  175:   const db = getDb();
  176:   const assignments = await db.task_assignments.where('task_id').equals(taskId).toArray();
  177:   const result: AssignmentSubmission[] = [];
  178:   for (const a of assignments) {
  179:     const subs = await db.assignment_submissions.where('assignment_id').equals(a.id).toArray();
  180:     result.push(...subs);
  181:   }
  182:   return result;
  183: }
  184: 
  185: export async function createSubmission(data: {
  186:   assignment_id: string;
  187:   submission_type: SubmissionType;
  188:   body: string;
  189:   submitted_by: string;
  190: }): Promise<AssignmentSubmission> {
  191:   const db = getDb();
  192:   const existing = await db.assignment_submissions.where('assignment_id').equals(data.assignment_id).toArray();
  193:   const maxNo = existing.reduce((max, s) => Math.max(max, s.submission_no), 0);
  194:   const ts = now();
  195:   const sub: AssignmentSubmission = {
  196:     id: uuid(),
  197:     assignment_id: data.assignment_id,
  198:     submission_type: data.submission_type,
  199:     submission_no: maxNo + 1,
  200:     body: data.body,
  201:     submission_status: 'submitted',
  202:     submitted_by: data.submitted_by,
  203:     submitted_at: ts,
  204:     reviewed_by_user_id: null,
  205:     reviewed_at: null,
  206:     review_comment: null,
  207:   };
  208:   await db.assignment_submissions.put(sub);
  209:   return sub;
  210: }
  211: 
  212: export async function updateSubmission(submissionId: string, updates: Partial<AssignmentSubmission>): Promise<void> {
  213:   await getDb().assignment_submissions.update(submissionId, updates);
  214: }

  323: 
  324: export function buildSubmissionViews(
  325:   submissions: AssignmentSubmission[],
  326:   users: Map<string, User>,
  327: ): SubmissionView[] {
  328:   return submissions.map((s) => {
  329:     const submitter = users.get(s.submitted_by);
  330:     const reviewer = s.reviewed_by_user_id ? users.get(s.reviewed_by_user_id) : null;
  331:     return {
  332:       id: s.id, assignment_id: s.assignment_id,
  333:       submission_type: s.submission_type, submission_no: s.submission_no,
  334:       body: s.body, submission_status: s.submission_status,
  335:       submitted_by_name: submitter?.name ?? '不明',
  336:       submitted_at: s.submitted_at,
  337:       reviewed_by_name: reviewer?.name ?? null,
  338:       reviewed_at: s.reviewed_at, review_comment: s.review_comment,
  339:     };
  340:   });
  341: }
  342: 
  343: export function buildAssigneeDetailViews(
  344:   assignments: TaskAssignment[],
  345:   users: Map<string, User>,
  346:   submissions: Map<string, AssignmentSubmission[]>,
  347:   requirements: Map<string, AssignmentDocumentRequirement[]>,
  348:   attachments: Map<string, TaskAttachment>,
  349: ): AssigneeDetailView[] {
  350:   return assignments.map((a) => {
  351:     const user = users.get(a.user_id);
  352:     const subs = submissions.get(a.id) ?? [];
  353:     const reqs = requirements.get(a.id) ?? [];
  354:     return {
  355:       assignment_id: a.id, user_id: a.user_id,
  356:       name: user?.name ?? '不明',
  357:       avatar_class: user?.avatar_class ?? 'avatar-navy',
  358:       initials: user?.initials ?? '??',
  359:       progress_status: a.progress_status,
  360:       assignment_status: a.assignment_status,
  361:       eligibility_status: a.eligibility_status,
  362:       is_complete: COMPLETE_STATUSES.includes(a.progress_status),
  363:       is_eligible_active: isEligibleActive(a),
  364:       completed_at: a.completed_at,
  365:       submissions: buildSubmissionViews(subs, users),
  366:       requirements: buildRequirementViews(reqs, attachments),
  367:     };
  368:   });
  369: }

  455: 
  456: export async function acceptWorkReport(taskId: string, submissionId: string, userId: string): Promise<void> {
  457:   const task = await getTask(taskId);
  458:   if (!task) return;
  459:   const ts = new Date().toISOString();
  460:   await updateSubmission(submissionId, { submission_status: 'accepted', reviewed_by_user_id: userId, reviewed_at: ts });
  461:   const subs = await getAllSubmissionsByTask(taskId);
  462:   const sub = subs.find((s) => s.id === submissionId);
  463:   if (sub) {
  464:     await updateAssignment(sub.assignment_id, { progress_status: 'accepted', completed_at: ts });
  465:   }
  466:   await addActivityEvent({
  467:     task_id: taskId, project_id: task.project_id,
  468:     event_type: 'work_report_accepted', label: '作業報告を受領しました',
  469:     user_id: userId,
  470:   });

  416: export async function confirmTask(taskId: string, assignmentId: string, userId: string): Promise<void> {
  417:   const task = await getTask(taskId);
  418:   if (!task || task.status !== 'open') return;
  419:   const ts = new Date().toISOString();
  420:   await updateAssignment(assignmentId, { progress_status: 'confirmed', completed_at: ts });
  421:   await addActivityEvent({
  422:     task_id: taskId, project_id: task.project_id,
  423:     event_type: 'submission_submitted', label: '確認を完了しました',

   17: export function isEligibleActive(a: TaskAssignment): boolean {
   18:   return a.assignment_status === 'active' && a.eligibility_status === 'eligible';
   19: }
   20: 
   21: export function computeBadge(task: Task, assignments: TaskAssignment[], requirements: AssignmentDocumentRequirement[]): DisplayBadge | null {
   22:   if (task.status !== 'open') return null;
   23:   const eligible = assignments.filter(isEligibleActive);
   24: 
   25:   // 1. 期限超過
   26:   if (task.due_at && new Date(task.due_at) < new Date()) return 'overdue';
   27: 
   28:   // 2. 差戻し対応中
   29:   if (task.purpose === '作業依頼' && eligible.some((a) => a.progress_status === 'returned')) return 'returned';
   30: 
   31:   // 3. 受領待ち
   32:   if (task.purpose === '作業依頼' && eligible.some((a) => a.progress_status === 'reported')) return 'accept_pending';
   33: 
   34:   // 4. 確認待ち (requirement_status=required exists)
   35:   if (requirements.some((r) => r.requirement_status === 'required')) return 'confirm_pending';
   36: 
   37:   // 5. 回答待ち
   38:   if (task.purpose === '回答・申込受付' && eligible.some((a) => a.progress_status === 'pending')) return 'answer_pending';
   39: 
   40:   // 6. 対応中
   41:   return 'in_progress';
   42: }
   43: 
   44: export function computeBoardColumn(task: Task, badge: DisplayBadge | null): BoardColumn {
   45:   if (task.status === 'draft') return 'draft';
   46:   if (task.status === 'closed' || task.status === 'archived') return 'closed';
   47:   if (badge === 'confirm_pending' || badge === 'answer_pending') return 'confirm_answer';
   48:   if (badge === 'accept_pending' || badge === 'returned') return 'accept_return';
   49:   return 'in_progress';
   50: }
   51: 
   52: export function computeProgressPercent(assignments: TaskAssignment[]): number {
   53:   const eligible = assignments.filter(isEligibleActive);
   54:   if (eligible.length === 0) return 0;
   55:   const completed = eligible.filter((a) => COMPLETE_STATUSES.includes(a.progress_status)).length;
   56:   return Math.round((completed / eligible.length) * 100);
   57: }

  139: export function formatDueLabel(iso: string): string {
  140:   const d = new Date(iso);
  141:   const now = new Date();
  142:   const diffMs = d.getTime() - now.getTime();
  143:   const diffHours = diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60);
  144:   const diffDays = Math.floor(diffHours / 24);
  145: 
  146:   if (diffHours < 0 && diffHours > -24) return '期限超過';
  147:   if (diffHours < 0) return `${Math.abs(Math.ceil(diffDays))}日前期限超過`;
  148:   if (diffHours < 24) return `今日 ${d.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${d.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
  149:   if (diffDays === 1) return '明日';
  150:   if (diffDays < 7) return `${diffDays + 1}日後`;
  151:   return `${d.getMonth() + 1}月${d.getDate()}日`;
  152: }
  153: 
  154: export function formatTimeLabel(iso: string): string {
  155:   const d = new Date(iso);
  156:   const now = new Date();
  157:   const diffMs = now.getTime() - d.getTime();
  158:   const diffMins = Math.floor(diffMs / 60000);
  159:   const diffHours = Math.floor(diffMs / 3600000);
  160:   const diffDays = Math.floor(diffMs / 86400000);
  161: 
  162:   if (diffMins < 1) return 'たった今';
  163:   if (diffMins < 60) return `${diffMins}分前`;
  164:   if (diffHours < 24) return `${diffHours}時間前`;
  165:   if (diffDays < 7) return `${diffDays}日前`;
  166:   return `${d.getMonth() + 1}月${d.getDate()}日`;

   44: export function computeBoardColumn(task: Task, badge: DisplayBadge | null): BoardColumn {
   45:   if (task.status === 'draft') return 'draft';
   46:   if (task.status === 'closed' || task.status === 'archived') return 'closed';
   47:   if (badge === 'confirm_pending' || badge === 'answer_pending') return 'confirm_answer';
   48:   if (badge === 'accept_pending' || badge === 'returned') return 'accept_return';

   47:   if (badge === 'confirm_pending' || badge === 'answer_pending') return 'confirm_answer';

   49:   return 'in_progress';
   50: }
   51: 
   52: export function computeProgressPercent(assignments: TaskAssignment[]): number {
   53:   const eligible = assignments.filter(isEligibleActive);
   54:   if (eligible.length === 0) return 0;
   55:   const completed = eligible.filter((a) => COMPLETE_STATUSES.includes(a.progress_status)).length;
   56:   return Math.round((completed / eligible.length) * 100);
   57: }

  391: // === Auto-close check ===
  392: export function checkAutoClose(
  393:   task: Task,
  394:   assignments: TaskAssignment[],
  395:   requirements: AssignmentDocumentRequirement[],
  396: ): boolean {
  397:   if (task.status !== 'open') return false;
  398:   const eligible = assignments.filter(isEligibleActive);
  399:   if (eligible.length === 0) return false;
  400:   const completeStatuses = COMPLETE_BY_PURPOSE[task.purpose];
  401:   const allComplete = eligible.every((a) => completeStatuses.includes(a.progress_status));
  402:   if (!allComplete) return false;
  403:   const pendingReqs = requirements.filter((r) => r.requirement_status === 'required');
  404:   if (pendingReqs.length > 0) return false;

   97:   '周知・意見聴取': 'purpose-notice',
   98:   '回答・申込受付': 'purpose-answer',
   99: };
  100: 
  101: function isTaskOverdue(task: TaskView): boolean {
  102:   if (task.status !== 'open') return false;
  103:   if (!task.due_at) return false;
  104:   return new Date(task.due_at).getTime() < Date.now();
  105: }
  106: 
  107: function formatDueAbsolute(dueAt: string | null): string {
  108:   if (!dueAt) return '期限なし';
  109:   const d = new Date(dueAt);
  110:   if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '期限なし';
  111:   const yyyy = d.getFullYear();
  112:   const mm = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  113:   const dd = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  114:   const hh = String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');

   44: export function computeBoardColumn(task: Task, badge: DisplayBadge | null): BoardColumn {
   45:   if (task.status === 'draft') return 'draft';
   46:   if (task.status === 'closed' || task.status === 'archived') return 'closed';
   47:   if (badge === 'confirm_pending' || badge === 'answer_pending') return 'confirm_answer';
